## Original-to-split result mappings

| Original target ID / file | Split result ID / file | Relation / exact scope | Source commit | Reason / history |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| M2 endpoint order in plan | PD-T017; PD-T018 | lower and matching upper inequalities, both PROVED | 2dc9264 | substantive separate statements; sharp constant PD-T020 now PROVED via PD-T546/523 |
| PD-T024 fixed-ratio MZ | PD-T022; PD-T023 | explicit upper and lower spectral intervals, both PROVED | 2dc9264 | original target remains PROVED by the two parts |
| M1 sharp strengthening | PD-T014 vs PD-T027 | strict threshold PROVED; sharper target PD-T027 now PARTIAL via PD-T526/530 | 2dc9264 | no silent narrowing of sharp target |
| M4 two-dimensional target PD-T028 | PD-T021 marginal compression | supporting reduction only; main target stays OPEN | 2dc9264 | signed-weight upper spectrum does not follow from compression |

## Programme-ideas campaign — final allocation (2026-11-01)

**Final IDs allocated 2026-11-01** by Claude under AC's explicit authorisation, recorded in the [batch ledger](topics/padua-batch-20261031/ALLOCATIONS.md): PD-T095–PD-T100, PD-N039–PD-N043, IDEA-190–IDEA-209, LIT-332–LIT-426. Provisional IDs are kept in parentheses or as aliases for provenance; the full map is [id-map.json](topics/programme-ideas-20261031/pr-prep/id-map.json).

Provisional theory/negative-result IDs of `claude/programme-ideas-20261031` (response-01 addendum, 2026-11-01). The 20 provisional IDEA IDs and the 111 provisional LIT IDs were added at PR preparation (2026-11-01). Their canonical files are `topics/programme-ideas-20261031/ideas/candidates.md` and `lit/baseline.md`. Two of the LIT IDs are merged into existing rows as the same statement, so they get no new ID; the deduplication is in `lit/integration-map.md`. After PR #10 merged, 14 more are merged into PR #10 rows (`lit/integration-map-pr10.md`). All other IDs received final IDs on 2026-11-01 (see above).

PD-T028 remains the original OPEN signed-Padua spectral target, supported by PD-T106/109/111/115/117 and finite PD-T107/108; endpoint upper target PD-T110 and fixed-ratio target PD-T116 remain OPEN. D5 uniform operator target PD-T114 remains OPEN; proved general tools PD-T112/113 and scoped failures PD-N050–052 are separate dependencies, not solved target conjuncts. Historical provisional PD-N?mz2d-codex-41 consolidates into PD-N045, with complete original proof bytes retained in the verification-only archive.

[PD-T028](tracks/mz-constants/theory/open-padua-target.md) →
[PD-T110](tracks/mz-constants/theory/mz2d-upper-open.md), the explicitly
identified upper-threshold part, **OPEN**; PD-T028 ↔
[PD-T116](tracks/mz-constants/theory/mz2d-fixed-ratio.md), a supporting
fixed-ratio target, **OPEN**, rather than a solved conjunct. In the reverse
direction, PD-T110's “Split from” names PD-T028; the original target and both
named targets remain OPEN. Supporting records are
[PD-T106](tracks/mz-constants/theory/mz2d-aliasing.md),
[PD-T109](tracks/mz-constants/theory/mz2d-defect-structure.md),
[PD-T111](tracks/mz-constants/theory/mz2d-lower-corner-weights.md),
[PD-T115](tracks/mz-constants/theory/mz2d-fixed-ratio-diamond-reconstruction.md),
[PD-T117](tracks/mz-constants/theory/mz2d-tensor-control.md), and the finite
[PD-T107](tracks/mz-constants/theory/mz2d-certified-spectra.md)/
[PD-T108](tracks/mz-constants/theory/mz2d-measured-spectra.md) records.
PD-T028's own file stays frozen because it is one of the 73 protected prior
MZ inputs. This crosswalk supplies the reciprocal administrative relationship
without changing that accepted record, its protected-object manifest, its
statement, or its status; supporting results are not proved target parts.

Original-to-split relations: PD-T140 (formerly carrying the finite inequalities) → **PD-T141** (finite computer-assisted PROVED, formal scalar model); PD-T140 keeps the MEASURED experiment, the INCONCLUSIVE both-grid sufficient test and its separately labelled factor-ten target, PROVED at frontier preparation (T24 conjunct PROVED in PD-T141(c), rotQ24 conjunct PROVED separately in PD-T331); historical Codex audit-03 PARTIAL is preserved, and the old experiment/sufficient test are unchanged. **PD-T075(U1) → PD-T144** (ρ_n = O(n^-5) for the named certificate); PD-T075 becomes PARTIAL with (U2) OPEN. PD-N062 is the generalized fixed-degree closure lemma and exact instance of PD-T055's recorded heuristic; PD-T055 stays OPEN. PD-N063 records three scoped failed negative-witness mechanisms. Permanent unused gaps: PD-T147–155, PD-N064–071, IDEA-268–285, LIT-445–459. Frontier split clarification: PD-T140 rotQ24 target conjunct → PD-T331 only. The fixed-old-lower failed IDEA IDEA-420 subroute → PD-N116; that negative record is not a split of the successful theorem or a rejection of the target. All other frontier supporting relations are listed in the separate section below.

map to PD-T485/486, distinct from uppercase P5 candidate aliases.

under IDEA-577; terminal-block and reflection reuse consolidate under

family prerequisites and abandoned rational-Padua substitute as method

`PD-T?...-NN` naming-template placeholder is not a candidate or allocation.

records; no equivalent/abandoned route creates an administrative PD-N.

Original-target/support relations: PD-T448 -> PD-T432/433/434/437 (supporting). PD-T448 is now PARTIAL: PD-T509 is its solved exact-benchmark Krawtchouk rate conjunct (see the [record](topics/nn-mop-stability-20261105/theory/conditioning-growth-target.md)). PD-T449 -> PD-T440/441/442 and PD-N163; each child retains its own statement and status. PD-T447 -> copy/pair sub-target PD-T446; PD-T445 is a supporting graph identity. Apart from PD-T509 for PD-T448, these are supporting results rather than solved conjuncts of the targets.

Original combined ratio-graph/copy negative alias PD-N?nn-mop-stability-codex-D6-literal is retained by PD-N167 (G). Its C statement was split into PD-N168, whose alias PD-N?nn-mop-stability-codex-D6-copy is separate. Finite Hahn application PD-T436 depends on generic criterion PD-T435 and is not a split-off conjunct of that theorem.

IDEA-532 and IDEA-536 consolidate the finite G3 screen and later fixed-N analytic proximity refutation under PD-N162 while retaining both candidate records. IDEA-534/535 are weaker/stronger witnesses within PD-T437, not duplicate theorem IDs. IDEA-537/538 share measured record PD-T440; IDEA-539 has separate proved geometry PD-T441 and measured fits PD-T442. IDEA-547 has separate positive encoding PD-T445 and scoped literal obstruction PD-N167; IDEA-548/549 share OPEN copy/pair sub-target PD-T446, with the no-deflation negative separately in PD-N168.

D2 retains its unnumbered known reorthogonalized-marginal and high-precision oracle comparators, deferred Gragg–Harrod/RKPW alternative, anchored path diagnostic and abandoned full-array interpretation in [its gate](topics/nn-mop-stability-20261105/novelty-gate-D2.md), linked to PD-T438/439. These considered subroutes create no administrative IDEA or PD-N IDs.

Unused IDs remain permanent gaps: PD-T450–471, PD-N169–177, IDEA-556–569 and LIT-615–629. LIT-567/568 are reused source identities; only LIT-614 is new.
